```
Thank you for reaching out about your order [order number]. Before we process a refund, we confirmed with the supplier, and the updated expected ship date for [product name] is [updated ship date].

We understand that waiting is frustrating. If you're willing to keep your order pending, we guarantee that your item will be among the first to leave the warehouse, and we can offer you a partial refund of [partial refund amount, e.g. 50 USD] as a thank-you. The amount depends on the order value.

If you would still prefer a full refund, just reply to this email and we will process it right away. We can also show you similar products that are in stock and ready to ship now: [links to similar in-stock products].

Please let us know which option you prefer.

Best regards,
[your name]
[store name]
```
[L1277.050]

#### Out-of-stock notice with similar products

Use this text on the product page for an out-of-stock item. (2023)

```
This product is currently out of stock and is expected back on [date] / has no clear restock date. Here are similar products we have in stock and ready to ship today: [product 1 link], [product 2 link], [product 3 link].
```
[L0835.036]

#### Confirming a backorder in the order email

- After a purchase of a backordered item, send an order confirmation email to the buyer asking them to confirm they saw the backorder note and accept the estimated ship date. (2024) [L0805.002]
- After the order comes in, manually email the customer to confirm the ETA for shipping. (2024) [L0666.028]
- If a customer still orders organically, email them after the order to confirm they accept the ETA. (2024) [L0965.034]

### Handling payment for backorders

#### Shopify settings without an inventory app

Use this procedure when no inventory app is installed. (2021)

1. Keep a product listed as in stock while it is available or expected back soon, and show an estimated arrival date on its page. (2021) [L0853.106]
2. Mark the item out of stock only when its backorder typically lasts a month or more and you do not want new orders. (2021) [L0853.106]
3. Set Shopify so checkout preauthorizes the customer's card without charging it. (2021) [L0853.106]
4. Capture the payment only after the supplier confirms the item is in stock. (2021) [L0853.106]
5. For items with a long lead time, do not capture the payment, which cancels the charge without a refund. (2021) [L0853.106]

### Pre-orders and made-to-order products

#### Pre-orders

- Pre-order sales are a workable approach when suppliers are out of stock. Reserve inventory for pre-order customers and set up delivery deals with customers so shipments are not missed. (2020) [L0967.036]
- Pre-order buyers should be informed of shipping timing and warehouse status. Warehouse closures in major hubs can delay shipping and require special arrangements with customers. (2020) [L0967.037]
- Standard stock-based fashion needs a lot of cash because stock must be bought up front. A pre-order model reduces this cash flow pressure. (Source C) [L0660.014]
- When a dropship supplier says stock will arrive in about a week, the seller can start running a pre-order or a "high demand, secure yours before stock runs out" message in the meantime. Ask the supplier whether stock is limited per batch or can be replenished continuously, and whether they will hold inventory for the seller. [L0552.013]

#### Made-to-order and custom products

- Sell made-to-order products only if they are made fairly close to the customer and customers are told about the lead time before ordering. Presenting the custom production as a benefit works better than presenting it as a delay. (2021) [L0962.024]
- Made-to-order furniture that ships after about 10 days was accepted by customers when the page explained that it was custom made locally. Customers were willing to wait for it. (2021) [L0962.025]
- Products with a long lead time, such as 16 weeks for made-to-order items, should be marked on the product page right by the add-to-cart button, with the production and delivery estimate. Taking full payment at the time of order is appropriate when the item is custom made. (older advice, 2022) [L1281.080]

#### Procedure: made-to-order item with a one-to-two-week production window

1. Put a note beside the add-to-cart button on the product page, quoting the supplier's exact phrasing, such as saying the item is made to order and dispatches within one to two weeks. (2024) [L0965.032]
2. Once a buyer places an order, email them by hand to get confirmation that they understand the build time. (2024) [L0965.032]
3. After the customer replies with their confirmation, forward the order to the supplier. (2024) [L0965.032]

Confirmation email for a made-to-order order:

```
Subject: Confirming your order [order number]

Hi [customer name],

Thank you for your order of [product name]. Just want to confirm that you're aware this product is built to order and ships in [lead time, for example one to two weeks].

Please reply to confirm, and we will send your order to production right away.

Thank you,
[store name]
```
[L0965.033]

#### Deciding whether to pre-sell

- If a supplier is out of stock and gives no restock date, do not sell or advertise that supplier's products. If restock is coming soon in a meaningful quantity, pre-selling can make sense; decide supplier by supplier. (2021) [L0968.010]
- Only pre-sell a backordered product when the supplier has given a reliable ETA, because customers will cancel or complain if a long backorder is not communicated. (2021) [L0942.010]

### Substitutions and replacements

#### Choosing a substitute

- If a supplier is out of stock but the product still shows as available on your store, offer customers compatible alternatives. This can turn orders that would have been lost into sales. Keep the availability status in mind when managing supplier inventory. (2025) [L0837.017]
- When an ordered product is out of stock, handle it in this order: 1) Order a similar product as a replacement. 2) If no similar product is available, find new suppliers that offer an alternative product. [L0920.031]
- When an ordered product goes out of stock, ship the in-stock items first and send the missing bundle component later. Suppliers usually restock accessories quickly, and customers accept the split if they got the deal. [L1357.042]
- Some dropshipping suppliers, when a product runs out, ship a different brand's product without asking the store owner. Check supplier practices early and set rules for substitutions, because this damages the brand and customer trust. (Source C) [L1315.009]
- For an out-of-stock product with no ETA, the replacement offer is the step in the procedure above (see L0965.030). [L0965.030]

#### Procedure: similar in-stock products on the product page

1. Cut paid ad spend for the product page. (2024) [L1115.012]
2. Add a note next to the add-to-cart button saying the item is out of stock and showing the ETA if one is known. (2024) [L1115.012]
3. Keep the page live so free organic traffic can still arrive. (2024) [L1115.012]
4. Below the note, show a section with similar products that are in stock and ship within 24 hours, so organic visitors can still buy. (2024) [L1115.012]

### Discontinued products

- When a supplier discontinues a product, keep its product page but mark it as discontinued. Show two or three similar products that are still in stock, and stop running ads to that page. (older advice, 2022) [L1281.011]
- Keep discontinued or unavailable products on the website as not orderable, with no ads, so they still pick up organic search traffic. (2020) [L1277.049]
- Products that are discontinued by a supplier are handled with the substitution steps above. [L1281.011]

#### Case: discontinued model with a phantom stock unit

A store received an order for a discontinued model, and the supplier's inventory system listed a stock unit that did not exist. The store explained the situation to the supplier's representative, who was not responsive at first. The store then renegotiated the price difference, got the old dealer cost, and charged the customer the new price. The store made more profit on that unit than it would have with the correct price. [L1472.029]

#### Case: a discontinued product on a government order

A government agency placed a sizable order, but the manufacturer had discontinued the product that same month, so the order could not be fulfilled as planned. Rather than cancel, the seller contacted two manufacturers who had earlier rejected them because they had not been in the industry long enough. Both agreed to fulfill the order. After the customer approved the change, the seller shipped a product of higher quality than the original, kept the customer as a repeat buyer, and added a new supplier with a new catalog. [L1357.041]

### Shortages and supplier reliability

- When a supplier runs short of stock, every seller who uses that supplier pushes the same limited inventory, so shift traffic and ad spend to suppliers that still have stock and strengthen those relationships. Suppliers that stay reliable during shortages tend to become the strongest long-term partners. [L0942.001]
- When supply is limited, work with the available suppliers, upload the approved products, and optimize them. Circle back to the other suppliers once supply normalizes. (2022) [L0963.089]
- If many product pages from one supplier are out of stock across different competitor stores, the supplier may be out of business, and it is worth reconsidering them. A single out-of-stock product is not a warning sign on its own. (2021) [L0853.114]
- Overseas dropshipping shipments can take several months to arrive, which damages trust and repeat purchases. Keeping your top 10 best sellers in stock locally, where possible, shortens delivery and reduces buyer hesitation. [L0390.058]
- In dropshipping, keep your top 10 best sellers in stock locally, if possible, to offer faster delivery to your best customers. This reduces the long wait that makes buyers hesitate. [L0390.028]
- Fitness and hobby niches had the longest supplier lead times around Q4 due to inventory and shipping congestion. Inventory is expected to normalize around Q1 of the following year. (older advice, 2021) [L1112.052]
- Chinese suppliers often have a large backlog and slow replies in the weeks after Chinese New Year. Plan ad spend and stock with delays of two to three weeks in mind. [L1173.029]
- Demand for home gym and workout equipment spiked during the 2020 COVID-19 lockdowns, and popular items sold out quickly. (See also the factory backlog note above.) [L0358.002]

### Price changes

- Price changes from suppliers are communicated by email to all retailers with the new prices and effective dates, so you normally do not need to check prices constantly. (2021) [L0834.020]
- Suppliers normally send price changes by email with an effective date, and product integrations for pricing or stock are often not available. Track price changes by watching supplier emails and their effective dates. (2025) [L0972.013]
- Suppliers usually announce price changes by email to their retailer list, stating the effective date and time and attaching the new price sheet. If a supplier changes prices without notice, ask them to include you in all future price-update emails so you can update prices before they take effect. (2025) [L0837.036]
- Most suppliers update pricing about once a year at most. They usually send a price sheet plus emails about new products or price changes, so weekly price checks are not needed. (2025) [L0966.028]
- Update product pages only when a supplier reports a change, such as a new size, SKU, variation or price change. Create a new product page for the new item and link to it from the old page. (2026) [L0808.003]

#### Procedure: new size, SKU, variation or price change

1. Wait for the supplier to notify you of a new size, SKU, variation, or price change. (2026) [L0808.003]
2. Create a new product page for the new item. (2026) [L0808.003]
3. Put a link on the old product page pointing to the new one. (2026) [L0808.003]
4. Update the old page as needed. (2026) [L0808.003]

### Pre-buying inventory

#### Principles and caution

- Do not pre-buy inventory from a supplier just to get a discount. Pre-buy only when your sales volume is high enough that you need to secure stock. (2020) [L0551.017]
- Buying inventory in advance only makes sense once you have a bankroll and a reliable sales history for a product. A common approach is to place a deposit before the busiest season such as Q4, while the supplier keeps the stock in its warehouse and continues to drop ship. (2021) [L0968.011]
- Pre-buying stock keeps inventory secured while you can still dropship the product. Other options are a third-party fulfillment center, which can get expensive, or your own warehouse, which makes you run a separate fulfillment business. (2026) [L0808.012]
- Do not ask a supplier to pre-stock inventory for a new store. Pre-purchasing inventory only makes sense for an established store with years of sales history with that supplier, checked before the Q4 peak season, and only for products where stock is running low. (2024) [L1115.008]
- Do not buy inventory to supply a marketplace such as Walmart. If you want to test it, list only a few brands whose suppliers reliably keep stock, instead of the whole catalog. (older advice, 2019) [L1069.014]

#### Pre-purchase before the holidays

- Pre-purchase inventory from a supplier you sell consistently when stock runs low, such as before the holidays; the supplier holds it in its warehouse and ships when customers order, avoiding the need for own warehousing. (older advice, 2022) [L0665.016]

#### Procedure: pre-purchased inventory for a best-selling product that sells out often

1. Estimate average monthly units sold, for example 20 units a month that mostly sell in the first 10 days. (2026) [L0808.011]
2. Ask the supplier to hold a higher monthly quantity in their warehouse, for example 40 units, and ship orders for you. (2026) [L0808.011]
3. Ask for a discount for ordering upfront. Suppliers often agree, since bigger orders give them faster cash and bigger manufacturer orders. (2026) [L0808.011]

### Q4 and holiday capacity planning

#### Why Q4 is the hardest part

- During Q4 the hardest part is not getting sales but delivering reliably and on time, since buyers are already in a buying mood. Stock availability and shipping time are the key bottlenecks. [L0780.005]
- During the holiday season, do not add products whose delivery before the holiday cannot be guaranteed. The lesson used mid-December as the cutoff for products that would not arrive in time. [L1172.015]

#### Procedure: Q4 logistics with a supplier-based approach

1. Do not jump on products that are already selling strongly without securing supply, because the supply often runs out first. [L0780.006]
2. Limit the number of product variants (e.g., reduce from five colors to two). [L0780.006]
3. Buy a small stock (roughly 50 to 100 pieces) and place it in a warehouse in China. [L0780.006]
4. With guaranteed stock you can predict supply, fulfill orders faster and guarantee shorter shipping times. The same advice applies to Chinese New Year. [L0780.006]

#### Weekly supplier calls and communication

- Schedule weekly Zoom or Skype calls with your supplier during Q4 planning and sales periods to verify fulfillment capacity, confirm inventory levels, and troubleshoot issues in real time. Missed communication during high-volume periods causes supply chain failures, leading to chargebacks and account suspensions. [L0309.005]
- A $500,000-revenue store was lost in one Q4 when the supplier quietly ran out of stock but did not communicate for 2-3 weeks. Daily sales of $3,000-$4,000 continued but no shipments left the warehouse. Customer chargebacks accumulated, payment processor terminated the account, and Facebook shut down the ad account due to complaint volume. [L0309.006]

#### Scaling capacity and event lead times

- Before scaling a product, have the supplier backend ready, including reliable suppliers and stock available to fulfill the orders that ads will generate. [L1469.007]
- Ask the supplier for the maximum number of orders they can handle comfortably per day, and use that number as the scaling threshold. Suppliers often over-promise; set the threshold lower than their stated maximum. [L1363.036]
- Ask the supplier how long processing takes for the actual order volume; processing time can rise sharply (in the lesson from about one week to much longer) during high demand. [L1363.037]
- Running out of stock during scaling causes angry customers and a fall in revenue. Plan supplier capacity before increasing ad budgets. [L1363.039]
- For date-specific events such as Valentine's Day, count the supplier's shipping time, around two weeks in one example. Start ads about four weeks before the event so the product arrives in time and refunds are avoided. [L1469.025]

### Mistakes that cost sales

- Running out of inventory means no sales and no income, so the seller must monitor stock levels closely. [L1153.050]
- Running out of stock during scaling causes angry customers and a fall in revenue. [L1363.039]
- Marking products as sold out with zero inventory while customers can still buy them can lead Google to suspend a Merchant Center account. [L0853.004]
- Some dropshipping suppliers, when a product runs out, ship a different brand's product without asking the store owner. [L1315.009]
- A supplier's system can show stock that does not exist, so do not rely on the inventory feed alone for an order. [L1472.030]

## Returns, Damages and Customer-Facing Supplier Issues

### Setting the Return Policy from Supplier Policies

- Your customer return policy should be the average of your suppliers' return policies. If most suppliers charge return shipping, the customer pays return shipping. (Older advice, 2020.) [L1277.036]
- Choose suppliers based on their return rules, and set your return policy per supplier or per brand. If a supplier accepts no returns, state clearly on those product pages that the item cannot be returned. (Older advice, 2020.) [L0664.017]
- Each supplier's return policy is written into its agreement and states how long returns are accepted and for which reasons. Check it per supplier before you offer refunds. [L0805.028]
- Returns are handled supplier by supplier, not by Shopify. Most suppliers offer a 30-day return policy, which is visible when you sign the supplier agreement. [L0666.031]
  - The supplier issues a return authorization label.
  - The item ships back to the supplier's warehouse.
  - The supplier credits the sale.
- Before you commit to a supplier with a higher return rate or heavy items, ask about its return policy and fulfillment options. Check whether it accepts returns at all, because a supplier that does not accept returns affects your own return policy. (Older advice, 2021.) [L0801.038]
- Many suppliers in China offer poor return policies, and returns often require the store to pay return shipping. Agree on return and size-change handling with the supplier before launch. [L0660.015]
- Returns can go back to a supplier abroad, but the store then pays the return shipping. A store with its own warehouse in the sales country can receive returns locally, though the goods must be flipped again. [L0660.017]
- Ask each supplier in advance whether it pays for a free replacement when an item is damaged in transit. Some suppliers cover the cost, while others only claim it from the shipping company. This changes how you handle damage claims. [L0352.060]

### Return Rate Benchmarks

Two sources from 2021 give different rules. Use the table to see when each applies.

| Rule | Value | When it applies | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Fixed target (older, 2021) | 1% or lower | Benchmark for every product. If returns consistently exceed it because of supplier packaging or quality, discontinue the supplier regardless of other factors. | [L0270.002] |
| Situation-dependent check (older, 2021) | No fixed percentage | Assess the product case by case, using the four factors below. | [L0270.061] |

Situation-dependent check, four factors:

1. How many units of that product have sold.
2. The root cause of returns: packaging damage, quality control, or customer expectation.
3. If returns are a problem from day one because of supplier quality, discontinue the supplier immediately.
4. If returns come from customer misunderstanding, fix the product page with clearer information. [L0270.061]

- Incorrect sizes raise the return rate, so a wrong size chart costs money through refunds and returns. Verify every size against centimeter measurements before you sell. [L0661.015]

### Free Returns and Restocking

- Offer free returns only when the item is defective or damaged, because then the supplier covers the cost. For other returns, match the restocking-fee norms of your niche. Avoid testing free returns before you have a cash reserve. (Older advice, 2020.) [L0468.048]
- If a supplier has no return policy, state on the product page that the item is not eligible for returns, except for damaged or defective items. (Older advice, 2020.) [L0468.049]

### Product Pages and Policy Pages

- In Shopify, product pages have tabs. The Shipping tab can show the specific supplier's return policy for that product, so make the supplier return policy unique on each affected product page. (Older advice, 2020.) [L1088.023]
- On the main Shipping and Returns page, state that the store's return policy excludes named suppliers. Then add each excluded supplier's own shipping and returns policy below that statement. (Older advice, 2020.) [L1088.024]
- Products from a supplier that does not accept returns can be marked on the product page as not eligible for return. The shipping and return policy can say that some exclusions apply and list them. (Older advice, 2023.) [L1094.016]
- The store's general shipping and return policy should combine its suppliers' policies. If a supplier does not accept returns, the product page says the item is not eligible for returns, and the policy page lists that exception below the general policy. (Older advice, 2023.) [L1114.022]
- When returns go to a third-party warehouse instead of the business address, the refund policy should state that the exact return address is given only after the return is authorized. Without this statement, Google may flag the site for hiding a dropshipping model. [L0724.026]

### Pre-Purchase Return Option (Checkout Plus)

- Checkout Plus is an unfulfillable product on the order. The main operational step is making sure the 3PL or warehouse understands this. No return label is included at purchase because of Checkout Plus. [L0955.029]
- Steps:
  1. Create the pre-purchase return option in Shopify as a product so it appears on the order. [L0955.055]
  2. Tell the 3PL or warehouse that this line is unfulfillable and must not be picked or shipped. [L0955.055]
  3. Confirm that the line is visible on the order for customer service staff. [L0955.055]
  4. Set a separate price per Shopify Market if the offer runs in several markets. [L0955.055]
  5. Do not include a return label at purchase because of the option. The label is issued only when the return is submitted. [L0955.055]

### Damaged Deliveries and Claims

- Before you sell a product, check whether its type is prone to shipping damage:
  1. Search Google for the brand names of your future competitors.
  2. Read their reviews and look for complaints that items arrived damaged.
  3. If many competitors have such complaints, assume the problem affects most suppliers in that industry. [L0769.011]
- Shipping damage depends more on the supplier and its packaging than on the niche itself. Industry-wide damage patterns can still show up in competitor reviews. [L0769.012]
- Claim routing for damaged deliveries (older advice, 2022). The shipment must be insured in both cases. [L1281.010]

| Who ships from its account | Who files the claim | What the seller still does |
|---|---|---|
| Supplier | Supplier, which processes the replacement or exchange | Handles the customer and the claim |
| Seller, with insurance | Seller, with the shipping carrier | Handles the customer and the claim |

- If a supplier repeatedly sends damaged goods, especially for fragile products, change suppliers rather than accepting the losses. [L1251.032]
- For a cheap product, the cost of goods plus shipping to the US can be about 7 to 8 USD per unit. Use this figure to decide whether a replacement or a partial refund costs less than a return. [L1128.008]

### Freight Deliveries and Refusals

- For damaged large items delivered by freight (older advice, 2021 to 2022):
  1. The customer photographs the damage on arrival.
  2. The customer notes the damage on the delivery receipt.
  3. The customer reports the damage to the carrier.
  - Carrier reporting applies to freight or LTL shipments, not to parcel carriers such as UPS, FedEx or USPS.
  - Large freight items require a signature on delivery, and the shipping company calls the customer to set a delivery time. [L0556.023]
- Freight deliveries require the buyer to be present to inspect the package and sign the receipt. If the outer packaging is clearly damaged, tell the customer to refuse the delivery. A refused shipment can be replaced by the supplier without repacking or return shipping. [L0667.012]
- For a freight shipment from a supplier, follow the freight carrier's guidance for damage claims and refusals. A damaged item that is accepted and then opened creates a much bigger return ordeal than one refused at delivery. [L0667.013]

### Supplier Errors and Substitutions

- Dropshipping means you have no warehouse of your own, so supplier mistakes land on you as refunds, complaints and reputational damage. Chinese suppliers may promise much and later deny the promise, so verify quality before you scale. [L0694.019]
- If a supplier ships a different product than the one you specified, the supplier is responsible for the mismatch. Before shipping, the supplier should send a photo or video of the product from the factory. Supplier quality varies by agent, so judge each agent on its own record. [L0694.018]
- A supplier that sends wrong materials, such as silver instead of gold, leads to negative reviews and can force a store to close. Confirm supplier quality before you scale. [L0492.021]
- Wrong or low-quality items from a supplier can create enough complaints and disputes that the brand's Facebook account and its ads are lost. Check samples and order accuracy before you increase ad spend on a new supplier. [L0492.066]
- Ask dropship suppliers to contact you before sending any replacement or substitute item when the original is out of stock. Suppliers may send a similar item or another brand's product to protect their own profit, which can damage your brand. Make this explicit in the agreement and in regular communication. [L0552.009]
- A dropship supplier substituting a different colour, variant or branded item for the ordered product can trigger chargebacks and customer complaints. In one case, around 300 units went out with another brand's label, which nearly crippled the business. [L0552.010]
- When a supplier is backlogged, ask it to ship the existing orders instead of issuing refunds, since refunds often create more problems. Resume ads only after the backlog is cleared or a new supplier is confirmed. [L1173.028]
- For an AliExpress order that needs a refund, the seller can ask AliExpress to refund the supplier payment. In the reported case the money came back almost immediately. Handle the buyer's refund separately, since the item may never need to return to the supplier. [L1128.031]
- Whether to cancel an order and request a refund depends on the relationship with the supplier. If the supplier appears to be stalling or lying, request the money back immediately. (Older advice, 2020.) [L0967.033]
- Long shipping times are less of a problem early on. Reply quickly to customer emails, apologize, give a reason such as customs or Covid delays, and offer a discount code for the next purchase to prevent chargebacks. [L0820.030]
- Case: A seller started a store with necklaces that had strong click-through rates. A buy one get one free offer produced around 60,000 within two months. The seller then switched to a random new supplier, which shipped the wrong product: gold necklaces arrived in silver, and some African-country orders went to the wrong country (Egypt orders received Ethiopia products). About 200 orders were affected. Some customers kept the product, others requested refunds, and the seller shut the store and restarted. [L0780.037]

### Supplier Packaging and Invoices

- Expect supplier boxes and product manuals to display the brand name and supplier information. This signals authenticity. Suppliers never include invoices or receipts showing your wholesale cost, because they do not sell directly to consumers. (Older advice, 2022.) [L0249.093]
- Authorized brands that do not sell direct to the public typically include only packing slips in shipments, not invoices or receipts. This is normal for authorized retailers and has not caused problems in almost 15 years. [L0806.025]
- Default supplier packaging shows the supplier's name, which is normal for dropshipping. Only worry if a packing slip or invoice contains the supplier's name. (Older advice, 2021.) [L0834.028]
- Shein's logo on a package is a branding problem when reselling Shein products, the same as with normal dropshipping. Branded dropshipping or a packaging solution is needed so the competitor's name does not reach the customer. [L1439.008]

### Return Procedures

- Supplier return handling (older advice, 2019):
  1. The customer contacts you by email or phone to request a return.
  2. Email the supplier rep with the order number and ask for a return authorization number.
  3. The supplier usually sends a shipping label directly to the customer.
  4. When the item arrives back, refund the customer.
  5. Ask the supplier to refund you. Return policies differ by supplier. [L1002.057]
- Return procedure for damaged or unwanted items (older advice, 2020):
  1. The customer reports the damaged or unwanted item by email.
  2. The store emails the supplier about that order and asks for a return shipping label.
  3. The supplier sends the return shipping label to the store.
  4. The store sends that label to the customer.
  5. The customer ships the product, and it goes directly back to the supplier's warehouse.
  6. Check each supplier's exact return process and policy during the approval process. [L1277.037]
- Handling a supplier return request:
  1. Send the supplier the order number and the return request. Ask for a prepaid return shipping label or the instructions the customer needs. [L0910.039]
  2. Set a reminder in your email tool to check for a reply after two or three days. [L0910.039]
  3. If there is no reply, follow up politely and ask the supplier to confirm that the return was handled. [L0910.039]
  4. Repeat the follow-up until the return is actually processed, because suppliers often delay returns. [L0910.039]
- Suppliers dislike processing returns even more than the store does. A return request should never be assumed to be done just because it was sent. The store is responsible for following up until the supplier acts. [L0910.041]

### Samples

- When customers ask for product samples, such as fabric swatches, ask the brand whether it will ship samples to your customers for you. Competitors may already do this, and the brand may ask you to pay the shipping. (Older advice, 2021.) [L0962.007]

## Dropshipping Platforms and Agents (CJ, Hyper Rescue, Zendrop, AliExpress)

### Platform roles and fit

- Source C recommends a fixed sourcing check order: (1) AliExpress, (2) CJ (Global Sources), (3) Best Dealer. [L0209.030]
- A 2021 Source A source (older advice) says not to source from AliExpress at all and to go direct to suppliers that stock products in the region you sell to, because this builds better supplier relationships and dropship approval. [L0270.042] The two positions conflict. The Source C order is the testing sequence, and the 2021 advice is the long-term direction.
- CJ Dropshipping is the default starting supplier for beginners, because it is the easiest to handle and the fastest to learn. Other suppliers can be explored once sourcing is understood, for better prices and shipping times. [L1108.027]
- Start with CJ Dropshipping for test products, then compare the others on offer, shipping times and convenience once sales grow. [L1171.034, L1171.055]
- Alternatives to CJ and Hyper Rescue are Zendrop and USA Drop, and mentors recommend different starting suppliers. Choose the one that fits your target markets and product types. [L1172.063]
- CJ Dropshipping and HyperSQ are suggested as the first fulfillment and sourcing tools for a beginner dropshipping store. CJ's sourcing price is the basis for calculating the selling price. [L1172.018]
- The default supplier suggested for beginners is CJ Dropshipping. Zendrop, HyperSQ or BestDealer can be used if they offer better shipping times and a lower price. [L1219.012]
- Common dropshipping suppliers for testing and scaling include CJ Dropshipping, HyperSKU, Zendrop, Best Dealer and AutoDS. [L1171.055]
- Supplier and dropshipping platforms named in the lesson include CG dropshipping, Hyper SQ, Source Inbox and Zendrop. Each has its own app that lets the store owner send order data to the supplier from a laptop or desktop. [L0760.004]
- Name spelling varies between units. HyperSQ is rendered for HyperSKU in the lesson, and the same company also appears as Hypersku and Hyper SKU. [L1171.034, L0552.011, L1017.031]
- Dropshipping suppliers can handle quality checks, packing and direct shipping, so the seller does not need to meet manufacturers or handle goods physically. [L0760.005]
- A fulfillment agent is necessary in dropshipping because it sources products directly from the factory and charges a small fee per order. This avoids the long delivery delays typical of AliExpress. [L1129.006]
- Fulfillment companies such as CJ or Hyper Rescue are a shared-support model. They suit beginners, and at scale dedicated support with real-time updates, better prices and customization becomes more valuable. [L0685.020]
- Dropshipping suppliers are not perfect. Their stock and return handling are often weak. Plan to move to private agents or your own warehouse once sales are high enough, and do not expect perfect service from a dropshipping supplier. [L1315.008]
- Dropshipping suppliers are cheaper to start with and slower to ship. Private agents tend to be cheaper with faster shipping once the store has volume. [L1315.011]
- Choose the supplier per product, because AliExpress and CJ Dropshipping are often close in quality and the better one can change by product. If Zendrop has the product at the same or lower price, Zendrop may be faster. If CJ offers more variety and better reviews, CJ is the better choice. [L0657.034]
- Dropshipping platform suppliers may not be good for the long term, and service depends on the product. If a product has no good manufacturer, switch to an agent or a better supplier. [L1440.014]
- Agents are third parties between sellers and direct factories. They have contacts with other agents and factories and can check whether a specific book is in stock in various locations, including provinces in China. [L0560.002]
- When a product is not sold on AliExpress or similar platforms, a sourcing agent in China can supply it. [L1184.071]

### Checking sourcing viability before committing

- Before committing to a product, check manually whether it is available on AliExpress to confirm sourcing viability. [L0209.031]
- Confirm that a supplier actually sources a product before building an ad or store around it. A sourcing agent may not carry marketplace items, so check availability with the supplier first. [L0524.032]
- Before ordering a product for testing, check whether it is available on a platform like Amazon for faster shipping and variation options. Direct orders from CJ Dropshipping were described as unreliable, with long delays and products not matching expectations. Ordering a single sample to check quality and allowing extra time for arrival is a reasonable precaution. [L0819.024]
- Before running ads, send the product to a supplier such as CJ to check whether it can be sourced. Ask the supplier whether it can be sold as a liquid, since liquids can have shipping restrictions. [L1171.036]
- Products on AliExpress can be found by searching the product name with the key words, which helps confirm sourcing options. [L1176.031]
- CJ Dropshipping sourcing request: it is free and easy to open, and it returns the supplier's actual price for a product. It is used to replace AliExpress estimates with accurate cost data. [L0693.020]
- If CJ does not offer a product, contact several other suppliers to request quotes. Compare them by how fast they reply and how good their English is. A reply that takes three to four days is a bad sign. [L0361.037]
- Choose a supplier by comparing product price, shipping time and service experience, and keep more than one supplier available. In the lesson's examples, CJ Dropshipping, Hyper SKU and Best Dealer were used alongside each other. A cheaper supplier is fine if it wins on these criteria. [L1017.031]
- Some AliExpress suppliers operate several shops and can combine different products into one parcel when they are ordered together. The lesson's operator used this so multi-item orders look like they come from one warehouse. Before listing many products from such a supplier, confirm how it consolidates shipments. [L1131.062]

### Quality verification

**Quality check before scaling a product (procedure)**

1. On AliExpress, look for a listing with a rating of 4.6 or higher and at least 50 reviews. [L0335.032]
2. If a listing has fewer than 50 reviews, treat it as doubtful and look for another listing of the same product. [L0335.032]
3. On Amazon, look for a rating of 4 or higher. [L0335.032]
4. Order one sample from the supplier before you scale. [L0335.032]

**AliExpress quality check (procedure)**

1. Search for the product term. [L1183.012]
2. Find a listing with at least 4.6 star rating and at least 100 orders. [L1183.012]
3. Avoid relying on listings with high order counts but a rating below 4.6. [L1183.012]
4. Record the AliExpress average rating in a column of the research sheet and use it to rank products. [L1183.012]

**Threshold conflicts**

| Check | Value | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Rating and review count | 4.6 or higher and at least 50 reviews; more reviews are better | L0335.032, L1436.011 |
| Rating and order count | 4.6 or higher and at least 100 orders | L1183.012 |
| Sold count and review count | Compare both; several hundred sales and reviews are more credible than about 16 sales | L1444.067 |
| Amazon rating | 4 or higher | L0335.032 |

- The review-count rule (50) is stated for AliExpress listings in one source and the order-count rule (100) in another. The sources do not say which applies in which case. [L0335.032, L1436.011, L1183.012]
- Several listings of the same product at 4.6 or higher and 50 or more reviews suggest acceptable quality. Ordering a sample is the surer check but takes time and is optional. [L1436.011]
- The same product can have several manufacturers, so one listing's rating does not describe the product as a whole. A listing rated 3.8 while another is rated 4.6 or higher suggests a weaker version of the product. [L0335.033]
- Compare the sold count and review count of AliExpress listings before choosing a supplier. A listing with several hundred sales and reviews is more credible than one with only about 16 sales. [L1444.067]
- Order and test the product yourself before promoting it with video or ads. If the product does not work as the content shows, buyers and commenters will find out and conversion will fall. [L1444.068]
- Check supplier reviews on AliExpress with care, because many suppliers there use fake reviews. Cross-check the product on Amazon, eBay or other marketplaces where similar products are sold. Read the negative reviews, since some are unreliable. For a final check, order the product yourself and judge its quality. [L1215.023]
- Ask an agent for videos and photos of each product part to check quality before you commit. Strong agents also work with the best suppliers and carry out quality testing. [L1217.023]

### Choosing a fulfillment partner

**Beginner recommendation**

- For beginners, the recommended fulfillment apps are CJ Dropshipping or Hyper Rescue, because they are more reliable and offer live agent support for refunds, returns and damaged packages. [L0635.005]
- Automation-only apps such as AutoDS lack real after-sales support. [L0635.005]
- Choose a fulfillment app with live agent support rather than a pure automation app, because refund, return and damaged-package cases are resolved through the supplier's agents inside the app. [L0635.012]
- CJ Dropshipping and Hyper Rescue both offer live agent support inside their apps for refunds, returns and damaged packages. CJ Dropshipping can also provide personal support once a store reaches good order volumes. [L0635.030]
- Start with one fulfillment provider such as HyperSQ. Switch to CJ Dropshipping or Zendrop if service quality is poor. The right provider depends on the product and on the support agent assigned to you. [L0363.020]
- Request quotes from three or four fulfillment companies before choosing one. Quotes typically arrive within 1-2 business days. Compare prices and the assigned support contact, not just the price per item. [L0363.019]
- A supplier typically assigns an agent to the seller, who can check orders, fulfillment problems and orders lost in transit. Using a dedicated agent contact is a way to resolve supplier issues quickly. [L0678.026]
- If a supplier's website gives only one support contact, post the question in the community chat and ask a mentor directly for their experience. Mentors often respond faster this way than through the supplier's support. [L1017.033]
- Choosing a supplier by product is a valid approach, and sources differ on the fit of each platform (see the table below). [L0657.034, L1172.063]

**Reported experience with named suppliers**

| Supplier | Reported experience | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Hyper Rescue | Recommended for beginners; strong quality, reliable shipping and personal support via WhatsApp or Skype | L0335.035 |
| Hyper Rescue | Considered more reliable in quality and shipping times than CJ in the mentor's experience | L0335.036 |
| CJ Dropshipping | Less reliable than Hyper Rescue in the mentor's experience (same source) | L0335.036 |
| CJ Dropshipping | Reported as reliable after a previous supplier sent wrong material | L0492.016 |
| CJ Dropshipping | Produced many undelivered orders and forced the seller to spend four to five hours per day on customer service | L1127.020 |
| CJ Dropshipping | Stock shortages reported by the speaker | L0552.011 |
| USA Drop | Poor experience reported by the speaker on variant accuracy | L0552.011 |
| USA Drop | Well regarded in the industry but not the cheapest | L1217.066 |
| Vio and Eprolo | Recommended for good communication and competitive prices; Eprolo was described as better than Vio | L1127.017 |

- Longer shipping times can still happen for a few orders even with good suppliers. [L0335.036]
- Choose a supplier with reliable delivery before you scale ad spend. [L1127.020]
- Choose the supplier that ships as fast or faster. If an AliExpress supplier ships as quickly as CJ, use the AliExpress supplier instead. [L1444.046]

**Other fulfillment companies**

- Zendrop and Source Inbox are fulfillment companies in the same category as CJ Dropshipping and Hyper Rescue. On platforms like these, the seller usually has to provide an AliExpress product link so the supplier can source the item. [L0635.029]
- Source Inbox offers live agents. [L1217.066]
- Other dropshipping fulfillment suppliers mentioned as alternatives to CJ Dropshipping are Best Dealer, Hyper Rescue and Zendrop, and AliExpress was mentioned as an option. [L0678.025]
- Dropshipping suppliers named in the course include CJ, Hypersku, Zendrop and USA Drop. Manufacturers from outside the dropship model require paying a minimum order quantity (MOQ) upfront, so they do not fit a pure dropship model. [L0552.011]
- Best Dealer, Zendrop and CJ were compared on price and shipping times across suppliers before one was chosen. [L1108.028]

### AliExpress: role and limits

- Older advice, 2019: AliExpress is not recommended as a long-term model because delivery takes too long, product quality varies, returns are hard to handle, chargebacks are common and prices cannot be controlled. Such a model can make money in the short term but is unstable for building a lasting store. [L0357.023]
- Older advice, 2021: AliExpress as a supplier source brings slow shipping, inconsistent quality, mostly low-cost goods, difficult returns and no brand loyalty. It does not build a lasting business. [L0469.028]
- AliExpress can be used only as a short-term supplier option. Move to better suppliers as soon as possible. [L0361.038]
- Avoid AliExpress as a direct fulfillment source because its shipping times are very long and its support is unreliable. Use a dedicated fulfillment company that connects to the store instead. [L0685.001]
- Sourcing from AliExpress requires a lot of manual work and gives little customer support. When a product is poor quality, AliExpress refunds are less reliable than those from dedicated dropshipping suppliers. [L1171.035]
- A cheap product sourced from AliExpress with slow shipping leads to angry customers and poor reviews. Do not copy a competitor who sells this way if you want a lasting brand, and check shipping times before you commit to a supplier. [L1176.047]
- Realistic AliExpress shipping was about 14 to 15 days in the session, which was considered acceptable. Delivery times of 4 to 6 weeks are a serious problem. [L1108.030]
- Direct AliExpress orders could take up to about two months. [L1428.017]
- Shein can potentially be used as a fulfillment source for dropshipped orders, especially for audiences on Facebook who are less aware of Shein. This was not yet tested by the speaker and needs to be verified before use. [L1439.007]
- Before reselling Shein products in a dropshipping store and ordering to varied addresses, contact Shein support first and explain the plan. Reselling without this step carries the risk of the account being banned. [L1439.009]
- A complete AliExpress rule set (older advice, 2021) is in the Source A sources, which prefer direct suppliers over AliExpress. [L0270.042, L0469.028]

### Importing products into Shopify

- AutoDS can import products from AliExpress and from CJ Dropshipping into a Shopify store. [L0363.083]
- Alternatively, a fulfillment company's own Shopify app, such as HyperSQ or CJ Dropshipping, can be installed to import products directly from that company. [L0363.083]
- To import products through a fulfillment app such as HyperSQ, install the app and send the AliExpress product link to their chat support, or source directly on their platform. Once they source the product, connect it to your store and the product list is created there. [L0363.018]
- CJ Dropshipping can import listings from AliExpress, so a product found on AliExpress can be fulfilled through CJ. Use this when a product is available on AliExpress but you want CJ as the fulfillment partner. [L1444.070]
- CJ Dropshipping can be used to source and fulfill products that were found on AliExpress. Pick the supplier that has the product in stock and connect it to the store as the fulfillment source. [L1439.006]
- Zendrop can be used to request or import a dropshipping product into Shopify. Alternatively, you can add the product link now and connect it to Zendrop later. [L1331.010]
- Older advice, 2020: Avoid product import apps that pull supplier catalogs into your store. They add a middleman between you and the brand, which reduces long-term profit compared with a direct relationship with the brand. [L0358.036]
- The 2020 advice (above) and the fulfillment-app guidance in this chapter apply to different stages. Import apps are the beginner route for testing, and direct brand relationships are the long-term goal. [L0358.036, L0635.005, L0270.042]
- Personalized products (for example engraved names) can be hard through CJ, which may require a developer to connect its API documentation. Print-on-demand providers such as Printful usually connect more easily to Shopify. [L1363.040]

**CJ Dropshipping: listing a product in Shopify (procedure)**

1. Search for the product on CJ Dropshipping, or search by keyword to find similar listings. [L0685.010]
2. Open the listing and select the specific variant (color and style). The variant changes the product price. [L0635.009, L0685.010]
3. Check the product price, the shipping cost and the total cost for the target country. [L0685.010]
4. Compare shipping options and pick the one with the best balance of low cost and reasonable delivery time (for example 7 to 15 days). [L0635.009]
5. Choose the List button, not Connect, for the product you want to add to the store. [L0635.009, L0685.010]
6. Set the store name, product type, collection, vendor and a target selling price. Set the Target Price (the selling price in the store), for example 39.99 USD. The vendor can be, for example, 'cj'. [L0635.009, L0685.010]
7. Click List it now. [L0635.009, L0685.010]
8. Wait 1 to 2 minutes for the product to appear in the Shopify store. [L0635.009, L0685.010]
9. Edit product title, images, descriptions and prices in Shopify as needed. [L0635.009, L0685.010]

- On CJ Drop Shipping, the Connect option adds the product to the store with images, videos and descriptions that can be edited. The List option is used when adding the product with a target price directly through the listing screen. [L0685.011]
- CJ Dropshipping has a very large catalog, so most products can be found and listed directly. [L0635.006]
- CJ Drop Shipping has a very large catalog, so most products found on AliExpress can also be found there. Products can be connected to a Shopify store within minutes. [L0685.003]
- CJ Dropshipping has a trending products page with product categories that is convenient for browsing dropshipping products. Its delivery times can be slow unless you pay for faster options. [L0706.031]

**Hyper Rescue: setup (procedure)**

1. In the Shopify App Store, search for Hyper Rescue, click it and install it. [L0685.014]
2. Create an account on Hyper Rescue. [L0685.014]
3. Browse the dashboard catalogs (for example Top pet supplies, TikTok viral products and niche categories) to search for products. [L0685.014]
4. If the product is not in the catalog, go to Smart sourcing or AliExpress sourcing. [L0685.014]
5. Copy the AliExpress link of the product, paste it into the field, and click Save. [L0685.014]
6. Wait about 1 to 2 days for sourcing to complete. [L0685.014]

- Hyper Rescue has a smaller catalog and works mainly through sourcing requests based on AliExpress links. [L0635.006]
- Hyper Rescue offers good shipping times, reliable support and better pricing, but it has a small catalog. Products not found in it are sourced by submitting an AliExpress link. [L0685.004]
- Hyper Rescue's dashboard offers preset catalogs such as top pet supplies, TikTok viral products and niche-down categories. If a product is not in the catalog, use Smart Sourcing or AliExpress Sourcing, or try an image search with the help of the live agent. [L0635.031]

### Sourcing products outside the catalog

**Hyper Rescue sourcing (procedure)**

1. Copy the AliExpress product link. [L0635.007]
2. In Hyper Rescue, open the AliExpress Sourcing section (or Smart Sourcing). [L0635.007]
3. Paste the link and click Save. [L0635.007]
4. Wait about 1 to 2 business days while the sourcing team finds the best factory, negotiates, obtains samples and verifies quality. [L0635.007]
5. Review the quote, which shows the unit price and shipping cost per country (US, Canada, Australia and UK differ). Check stages such as 'sourcing in progress' and 'quote created'. [L0635.007]
6. In Hyper Rescue's AliExpress sourcing, a submitted product moves through status stages: first 'sourcing in progress', then 'quote created'. The quote shows the price per unit and the shipping cost for each destination country, such as US, Canada, Australia or UK. Check the quote for your target country before accepting it. [L0685.026]

**Request sourcing for a product not in the catalog (procedure)**

1. Try a keyword search first. If there is no match, use image search with a product photo. [L0685.017]
2. If the product is still not found, contact the live agent support. [L0685.017]
3. Alternatively, open the Sourcing section and click Post sourcing request. [L0685.017]
4. Open the sourcing request section and choose Post Sourcing Request (Hyper Rescue) or the equivalent request option. [L0635.008]
5. Enter the product title, a target price, and the AliExpress link. [L0635.008]
6. For a product already in the Shopify store that should be sourced from another provider (for example from Hyper Rescue to CJ), select that product from the store and submit the request. [L0685.017]
7. For a product that exists only as an AliExpress link, click the product request, enter the title (for example Cup washer), enter the target price, paste the AliExpress link and submit the request. [L0685.017]
8. Submit the request. Sourcing takes about 1 to 2 days. [L0635.008, L0685.017]
9. If neither keyword nor image search works, the live agent can help. [L0635.008]

**CJ sourcing request (procedure)**

1. Submit a sourcing request on CJ with the link to the product, for example an AliExpress listing. [L1428.016]
2. CJ contacts the producer or supplier and checks that the product is available for your store. [L1428.016]
3. Once a customer orders, you pay CJ for that order. [L1428.016]
4. CJ arranges the supplier's shipment, customs clearance and delivery to the end customer. [L1428.016]
5. Check product quality yourself, because CJ's sourcing does not guarantee it. [L1428.016]

**Sourcing request via CJ (procedure, short form)**

1. Find the product on CJ. [L1108.033]
2. If it is not listed, submit a sourcing request with the AliExpress product link. [L1108.033]
3. Wait for the answer, which is given within 24 hours and states whether the product is available. [L1108.033]

- CJ is a fulfillment provider and not the manufacturer. Its higher unit price pays for handling, shipping, customs and delivery. The reported average CJ delivery time was 7 to 15 days, while direct AliExpress orders could take up to about two months. Choose the fulfillment route based on how much delivery time matters to your customers. [L1428.017]
- CJ Dropshipping can be used to source and fulfill products that were found on AliExpress. [L1439.006]
- A CJ product that is not in the catalog can be requested through a sourcing request. [L1428.016]
- The sourcing request on CJ is free and easy to open. [L0693.020]
- Negotiate and request in the live chat with a human agent (see the pricing section). [L1217.016]

### Shipping lines, processing and delivery times

**Uniexpress shipping lines**

| Line | Description | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Uniexpress Express | Best quality, most expensive | L0298.018 |
| Uniexpress Standard | Most used by reliable agents, under 9-10 days | L0298.018 |
| Uniexpress Economical | Cheapest, slowest | L0298.018 |
| China Post Standard | Avoid; cheaper alternative | L0298.018 |

- With stock on hand and Uniexpress Standard shipping, total delivery time is under 9 days. [L0298.020]
- CJ Dropshipping assigns a default shipping line at order creation, usually the cheapest. You can change it when you fulfill each order in the CJ order section (Add to cart and fulfill), and the choice is asked again for every order. Pick the fastest line whose price is within about one or two dollars of the cheapest. [L1218.004]
- When choosing a shipping option at CJ Drop Shipping, pick the option that is both cheapest and has good delivery times. For the US example, CJ Packet Ordinary with 7 to 15 days delivery was chosen. [L0685.007]
- Using the UN Express Priority shipping line from a China-based agent delivers in about 7 days, while FedEx and DHL are more expensive. [L0363.023]
- Shipping option check: on the CJ Dropshipping website, open the chat option in the left-hand menu and click the orange icon to reach a live agent. In the account section, check for an option that lets you select which items ship together for multi-item upsell orders. If none exists, ask the live agent to combine them from the same supplier. [L1217.068]

**Processing and delivery time conflicts**

| Context | Processing | Shipping / total | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Generic dropshipping | 2-5 days normally | Not stated | L0298.019 |
| Stock on hand, large volume (10+ orders/day) | 12 hours | Under 9 days with Uniexpress Standard | L0298.019, L0298.020 |
| Standard fulfillment companies | 1-2 days, sometimes 3-4 | Not stated | L0685.024 |
| Private agent holding stock | 12-24 hours | Not stated | L0685.024 |
| Order processing from China (typical) | About 2-3 business days before shipping | Under about 12 days total is considered good | L0363.022 |
| CJ Dropshipping (stated) | 2-3 business days, longer at high volume | Not stated | L1218.006 |
| CJ Dropshipping (reported) | About three days | About two weeks; products listed as 7 or 14 business days | L1444.045 |
| CJ Dropshipping (seller's experience) | Additional 8-12 days | Listed about 21 days; total close to one month; some orders up to 45 days | L0678.022 |
| CJ Dropshipping (listed) | Not stated | 7-15 days considered decent | L0635.011, L1428.017 |
| CJ Dropshipping (UK) | Not stated | About 6-7 days to the UK | L1218.005 |
| CJ Dropshipping (US and Australia) | Not stated | Roughly equal times to each other; similar to European countries | L1218.005 |
| AliExpress (realistic) | Not stated | About 14-15 days, acceptable in the session | L1108.030 |

- Order processing from China typically takes about 2-3 business days before the parcel ships, because items are moved to a warehouse, checked, stripped of Chinese labels and packed. [L0363.022]
- CJ Dropshipping's warehouse is typically in Shenzhen, and the product must first travel from the manufacturing city, which adds time. [L1218.006]
- Two or three large Chinese shippers, including YunExpress and Yun When Express, are the fast lines for the US market. [L1218.005]
- Long delivery times from a China-based agent can come from where the agent is located, because parcels from some provinces pass through several cities before reaching the export airport. Ask where the supplier or agent is based before promising delivery times. [L1214.001]
- For China-based fulfillment, Guangzhou and Shenzhen were named as the strongest provinces because they process goods faster on the way to the airport. [L1214.002]
- Slow agent shipping can damage influencer campaigns, so check delivery times before promoting the product through influencers. [L1184.071]
- Check shipping times before you commit to a supplier. [L1176.047]
- Products should be checked for delivery window. Shipping was listed as 7 to 15 days for the sample product. [L0685.008]
- Some orders in the example took up to 45 days, which made it unacceptable for the seller. [L0678.022]
- Customers accepted the CJ delivery times of about two weeks. [L1444.045]
- Treat an order that is not shipping within your target window as a delivery problem and move the product to another route if it continues. [L0678.024]

**Tracking and order status**

- Shipping status shows in the order list at each stage in the integrated app, such as 'sourcing in progress' and 'quote created' for sourcing requests. [L0685.026, L0635.007]
- A supplier agent can check orders, fulfillment problems and orders lost in transit. [L0678.026]
- Use the agent contact to resolve tracking or lost-order cases quickly. [L0678.026]

### Order fulfillment

**CJ Dropshipping: fulfilling store orders (procedure)**

1. Open the My CJ section. [L0685.013]
2. Open Imported orders. [L0685.013]
3. Shopify orders are imported into the My CJ section. Click Sync Store Orders (or Sync store orders) to pull all Shopify orders into CJ. [L0635.010, L0685.013]
4. Open each order and click Fulfill (or Add to cart) to fulfill it. [L0635.010, L0685.013]

**Hyper Rescue: fulfilling store orders (procedure)**

1. Orders from the Shopify store sync automatically into the Order list in Hyper Rescue, and new orders appear dynamically. [L0635.010, L0685.018]
2. Open the order and click Fulfill. [L0635.010, L0685.018]
3. Pay for the product price plus shipping cost. [L0635.010, L0685.018]
4. Hyper Rescue handles the rest of the fulfillment. [L0685.018]

**Multi-item orders**

- When a store has multi-item orders from upsells, ask the live CJ agent to ship items from the same supplier together. CJ may otherwise send them in separate packages from different manufacturers. [L1217.024]
- At good upsell volume, switch to an agent. Agents can combine packages and customize orders, which dropshipping platforms typically do not. [L1217.025]

**Costs per order**

- CJ Dropshipping example: a cup washer costs 5.95 USD for the chosen variant. The total cost including shipping to a US address is 18.85 USD. The price range across variants runs from 1.12 to 10.75 USD. Shipping times of 7 to 15 days were considered decent. [L0635.011, L0685.008]
- CJ product prices vary by variant, ranging from 1.12 to 10.75 USD for the same listing. Check the price of the exact variant before listing. [L0685.009]
- A product sold at 6.45 USD with US shipping had a delivery window of 7 to 15 days. A specific variant (color or style) cost 5.95 USD for the product, with a total cost of 18.85 USD including shipping to a US address. [L0685.008]
- Pay the fulfillment provider pay-as-you-go with PayPal or credit card. It has no monthly plan. You can top up a balance (for example 200-300 USD) and then click fulfill per order. [L0363.021]
- Faster suppliers can cost roughly 10% more per product than slower ones. [L0678.025]

### Pricing, negotiation and agent selection

- Supplier pricing does not automatically decrease in quality. If a supplier cannot lower price further, it will say so. Volume commitments, especially stock, provide negotiation leverage. [L0298.023]
- Negotiate in the live chat of the supplier with a human agent, ideally during Chinese working hours. Automated replies rarely offer discounts, so get a live agent first. [L1217.016]
- Listed prices on platforms such as Hyper Rescue and CJ are often higher than what you can get through direct negotiation. Do not accept the listed price without asking a live agent for a discount. [L1217.018]
- Platforms and agents such as CJ Dropshipping and Hyper Rescue often list higher prices to new dropshippers, because they prioritize long-term partners who bring steady volume. Negotiate by live chat and expect lower prices than the listed price. [L1217.065]
- Listed prices are negotiable through live chat for CJ Dropshipping and Hyper Rescue. [L1217.066]
- Play agents against each other by sharing a competing agent's quote in chat and asking for a better offer. Ask each agent for a quote that beats the others to get the best price. [L1217.021]
- Suppliers and agents do not mind when you send the same inquiry to several of them. They compete for good long-term clients, so the goal is to be a strong client to whoever you choose. [L1217.022]
- Once a store reaches a consistent daily order count, suppliers may negotiate price and pre-order a week of inventory into their warehouse. [L1315.011]
- Request stock inventory for at least one week when reaching 10+ daily orders. Immediately stop shipping new items from China to enable faster stock-based fulfillment if a customer cancels. [L0298.021]
- Order stock starting at 10+ orders per day to save 0.5-1 dollar per item and reduce processing time to 12 hours (vs. 2-5 days normally). [L0298.019]
- A dedicated fulfillment agent or personal agent can prioritize your orders at CJ Dropshipping when the default processing times are not reliable. [L1218.007]

**Asking for better service with no sales history (procedure)**

1. State that you have sold before and that this is a new store you are testing with products. [L1440.013]
2. Say that you want a long-term partnership. [L1440.013]
3. Ask for better quotes and explain that you have experience. [L1440.013]
4. Ask for a personal agent. The platforms provided one by Skype. [L1440.013]
5. Send consistent sales, because suppliers give better service to customers who bring steady business. [L1440.013]

**Negotiation template (verbatim)**

```
Hello [Name], I am an experienced dropshipper and have built multiple six-figure stores. I am now starting a new project in the [niche] niche, and I am looking for a long-term partnership rather than a one-off order. My current agent quotes [current price] for [product] at [volume] orders per month. If you can match or beat that price and offer a good discount for [product] at [volume] orders per month, I would like to move forward with you. Please tell me what minimum volume you need to offer your best rates.
```
[L1217.017]

- Negotiating with the 1688.com price: 1688.com is a Chinese domestic wholesale platform that shows the actual price suppliers pay. The site is in Chinese, so translate it and compare that price with your agent's quote to see the markup being charged. [L1217.015]
- Personal agents usually require a minimum of about 5-10 orders per day. Move to a personal agent once your volume reaches that level. [L1217.019]
- Ecom Ops requires a minimum order quantity of about 100 units per item. Other agents can work with volumes like about 5 orders per day or around 100 orders per month. [L1217.020]

**Cost comparison (example)**

| Option | Product | Shipping | Total | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Fulfillment company | 20 USD | 10 USD | 30 USD | L0685.022 |
| Private agent (same product and shipping) | About 20-25 USD (combined) | Included | About 20-25 USD | L0685.022 |

- Cost comparison: a fulfillment company may charge 20 USD for the product plus 10 USD for shipping, a total of 30 USD. A private agent may deliver the same product and shipping for about 20 to 25 USD. [L0685.022]
- Choosing between two suppliers with the same product cost but different shipping times (procedure):
  1. If the faster supplier with 6 to 10 days delivery charges much more, the slower one is worth considering. [L1216.021]
  2. If prices are similar, choose the supplier with 6 to 10 day shipping over the one with 12 days. [L1216.021]
  3. A two-day difference in delivery time is not a problem if customer support is good. [L1216.021]
  4. To settle it, split orders about 50/50 between both suppliers and compare customer experience. [L1216.021]

### Volume thresholds for moving to private supply

| Trigger | Threshold | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Begin using a fulfillment agent (for example Unified Dropshipping) | 20-30 orders per week; contact via Skype with referral details for best pricing and service | L0309.018 |
| Order stock | 10+ orders per day | L0298.019, L0298.021 |
| Consider switching to a private supplier or agent | Consistently 10 or more orders per day; start with a fulfillment company while sales are zero or low | L0685.019 |
| Own supplier offered | Roughly 10 orders per day for about one to two weeks; until then use a standard fulfillment app | L0820.033 |
| Personal agent minimum | About 5-10 orders per day | L1217.019 |
| Move to a private supplier | At least about 5 orders per day, or 3-4 days of steady sales; the private supplier must reply within its working hours | L0363.024 |
| Agent accepts accounts | Some agents only take consistent order volume, around 5 or more orders; others accept new sellers directly | L1218.007 |
| Other agents | About 5 orders per day or around 100 orders per month | L1217.020 |
| Ecom Ops | About 100 units per item (MOQ) | L1217.020 |
| Upsell volume | Switch to an agent at good upsell volume (no figure given) | L1217.025 |
| Renegotiate supplier price | Only once demand is consistent | L1426.033 |

- Use a standard fulfillment app early on and do not chase your own supplier. [L0820.033]
- Once the store has consistent demand, the store should contact other suppliers and negotiate a better price. [L1426.033]
- Start with a fulfillment company while sales are still at zero or low. [L0685.019]
- Shared support from fulfillment companies like CJ or Hyper Rescue is fine for beginners. [L0685.020]
- Processing time comparison: with standard fulfillment companies, the factory is contacted only after an order arrives. Processing usually takes 1 to 2 days, sometimes 3 to 4 days. With a private agent holding stock, processing can take 12 to 24 hours. [L0685.024]

### Private suppliers and agents (Source D setup)

**Who to contact**

- The lesson lists four private suppliers for order fulfillment: Bonnie, John, Veddo (spelled Vito in the lesson page text) and Joelle. Their WhatsApp contacts are on the course page and are not repeated here. [L1156.001]
- Make first contact with John or Joelle, who are the recommended private suppliers. Use Bonnie or Veddo only as a fallback if John and Joelle do not carry the product. [L1156.002]
- Contact private suppliers by WhatsApp direct message, using the number from the supplier list. [L1156.003]
- For digital products such as books, John and Joelle are the most suitable suppliers, because PDF versions of the content can be sourced from the internet. [L1156.013]
- The private supplier contacts on the course list use international phone prefixes (+86 and +852), which points to suppliers based in mainland China and Hong Kong. Confirm the supplier's location and shipping terms before committing, since the target market is usually elsewhere. [L1156.014]

**Message template (verbatim)**

```
Hello, I'd like to dropship this item: [PRODUCT NAME]. Photo of the product attached: [Attach product photo]. Could you verify whether this precise product can be supplied with no minimum order quantity (MOQ) and delivered to [COUNTRY, e.g. the USA]? I also need your price per unit, the shipping charge, and how long order processing takes. [Optional: referral line, only if someone referred you to this supplier, e.g. 'X sent me your contact'] Kindly share your contact information so we can move forward. Thank you.
```
[L1156.004]

- Send the supplier a product image in the first message so they know exactly which product you mean. [L1156.007]
- Before committing to a private supplier, confirm there is no minimum order quantity and that the supplier ships to your target country. [L1156.008]

**Setting up a private supplier (procedure)**

1. Pick John or Joelle first. If neither carries the product, pick Bonnie or Veddo. [L1156.005]
2. Get the supplier's WhatsApp number from the supplier list. [L1156.005]
3. Send the WhatsApp direct message using the template, with a product photo attached. [L1156.005]
4. Make sure the supplier confirms the exact product is available, that there is no MOQ, and that they ship to your target country. [L1156.005]
5. Once the supplier agrees, go to shopify.partners and give the supplier partnership access to your Shopify store, following the instructions the supplier sends you. [L1156.005]
6. The supplier fulfills the orders from your store. [L1156.005]
7. Pay the supplier a fixed amount per order via Wise or bank transfer. [L1156.005, L1156.011]

**Why and how private suppliers differ**

- Private suppliers are used instead of public dropshipping apps to get better margins, custom branding and more control over the product and fulfillment. [L1156.006]
- With a private supplier, the supplier handles fulfillment of orders from your store. Your role is to grant store access and pay the supplier per order. [L1156.009]
- Pay private suppliers a fixed amount for each order they fulfill, using Wise or bank transfer. [L1156.011]
- The supplier's ship-from location and processing time are the first things to confirm (see the processing table above). [L1214.001, L1156.014]

### Zendrop

- Zendrop's Select plan offers a no-questions refund on returned or refunded items. After you refund the customer and send proof such as a screenshot, the supplier refunds the product cost. Ask other suppliers for similar terms if they do not offer it on their basic plans. [L0335.037]
- Zendrop contacted a store doing 30-50 orders per day and offered to match or beat supplier pricing. Suppliers track connected app installations to identify high-volume sellers for recruitment, and Zendrop and similar platforms use this data to proactively reach out. [L0298.016, L0298.017]
- Zendrop can be used to request or import a dropshipping product into Shopify. Alternatively, you can add the product link now and connect it to Zendrop later. [L1331.010]
- Zendrop is an option for single-order dropshipping. [L1217.066]
- Zendrop and USA Drop are other dropshipping suppliers mentioned as alternatives to CJ Dropshipping and HyperSQ. [L1172.063]

**Case: Zendrop's recruitment and the switch back to CJ (case)**

- Situation: a store doing 30-50 orders per day was contacted by Zendrop, which offered to match or beat supplier pricing. [L0298.016]
- Initial appeal: low prices, a private Slack group and multiple agents. [L0298.016]
- Problem: the appeal faded when service quality proved poor. Shipping lines were cheaper, deliveries were late, agents were not responsive, and shipping costs doubled compared with CJ. [L0298.016]
- Outcome: CJ maintained competitive pricing when shown competitor quotes and provided superior service and fulfillment times. [L0298.016]

### Other platforms and suppliers

**Big Buy and Spocket**

- Big Buy is a European all-in-one dropshipping platform with stock in Spain and Germany that integrates with Shopify. It ships with UPS, FedEx and DHL, so its shipping costs are higher than those of typical China-based suppliers. [L1214.004]
- Big Buy accepts sourcing requests, where the company finds products that are not in its catalog through EU suppliers. Check its current catalog before relying on it, since the website had changed a lot after 2021. [L1214.006]
- A US version of Big Buy exists, but the European version was rated better for sellers focused on Europe. [L1214.007]
- Spocket offers a free trial of about three days. Use it to check whether the products you need are listed, and cancel if they are not. [L1214.009]

**Other suppliers**

- Eprolo was described as better than Vio, and a factory that made the products moved to Eprolo because it wanted to work in dropshipping style. [L1127.017]
- Day One is a large agent supplier that high-volume dropshipping stores use for fulfillment. Before switching to it, request a quotation and expect that it will need access to the store, so close out any unfulfilled orders on the old supplier first. [L1173.047]
- AutoDS can import products from AliExpress and from CJ Dropshipping into a Shopify store. [L0363.083]
- A Shein-based fulfillment option (not tested by the speaker) should be verified before use. [L1439.007]
- Best Dealer was used alongside CJ Dropshipping and Hyper SKU. [L1017.031]
- Best Dealer is named as an alternative. Compare prices and shipping times across suppliers before choosing one. [L1108.028]

### Switching suppliers and leaving a platform

- Supplier path for dropshipping (procedure):
  1. Start with CJ Dropshipping for product testing. [L1171.034]
  2. Once you make significant sales, switch to a supplier with better offers and shipping times such as HyperSKU (rendered as HyperSQ in the lesson), Best Dealer or a private agent. [L1171.034]
  3. Choose the supplier that gives the best offer, shipping times and convenience at each stage. [L1171.034]
- Move from a fulfillment platform to a private supplier once you have consistent demand, for example at least about 5 orders per day or 3-4 days of steady sales. The private supplier must reply within its working hours. [L0363.024]
- Keep the supplier that works best for each product, and consider using different suppliers for different products in the same store. [L0678.024]
- Using two different suppliers in one store is possible, with a clear overview of which orders go to which supplier. Products can be split between suppliers depending on which one ships them fastest. [L0678.027]
- Start with an easy-to-set-up supplier such as CJ Dropshipping, track processing and shipping times per order, and test other suppliers if delays become too long. [L0678.024]
- A dropshipping supplier such as CJ Dropshipping is acceptable for early testing because it is easy to set up and offers workable prices and shipping. Only once demand is consistent should the store contact other suppliers and negotiate a better price. [L1426.033]
- The right provider depends on the product and on the support agent assigned to you. [L0363.020]

**Switching checklist (procedure)**

1. Before switching suppliers, stop running ads on the date the old supplier will stop receiving orders. Orders placed after this date may stay unprocessed and cause refund and delivery problems. [L1173.026]
2. Check that all paid orders on the old platform are marked as fulfilled in Shopify. Unmarked orders can be paid for twice when the new supplier ships them. [L1173.027]
3. For Day One specifically, request a quotation first. Expect that it will need access to the store, and close out unfulfilled orders on the old supplier before you connect it. [L1173.047]

**Choosing between suppliers and the cost of switching**

- Choose the supplier that ships as fast or faster. [L1444.046]
- Products can be split between suppliers depending on which one ships them fastest. [L0678.027]
- Choose the fulfillment route based on how much delivery time matters to your customers. [L1428.017]
- Choose a supplier with reliable delivery before you scale ad spend. [L1127.020]
- If a product has no good manufacturer, switch to an agent or a better supplier. [L1440.014]
- Dropshipping suppliers are cheaper to start with and slower to ship, while private agents tend to be cheaper with faster shipping once the store has volume. [L1315.011]

### Customization, bundles and complementary products

**Complementary product (procedure)**

1. Search for accessories in the product's niche (for example fitness, jewelry, hair or nails accessories). [L1345.023]
2. Apply the price filter with a minimum of $0.01 and a maximum of $4. [L1345.023]
3. Pick an item that is light and adds little to shipping cost, and that complements the main product. [L1345.023]
4. Add it to the store as a bundle component. [L1345.023]

- Personalized products (for example engraved names) can be hard through CJ, which may require a developer to connect its API documentation. Print-on-demand providers such as Printful usually connect more easily to Shopify. [L1363.040]

### Testing products

- Explore products on CJ Dropshipping and AliExpress when TikTok search yields no good options. Buy the product you find promising and test it by creating videos about it. Use the test results to decide whether to continue promoting the product. [L1384.044]
